# Gate Audit Index

Canonical location for generated gate registry audits.

Rules:

- Gate audits must not live in the repository root.
- New generated gate audits should be written here as `gateNNN_registry_audit.md`.
- Missing numbers are allowed when no audit was generated for that gate.

Total audits indexed: 316

Known missing audit numbers in the current range:

```text
191, 192, 198, 324, 329, 360, 388, 389, 390, 391, 392, 426, 427, 428, 429, 430, 431, 432, 433, 434, 435, 436, 437, 438, 439, 440, 441, 442, 443, 472, 479
```

### Gate 585 — Koide Wall-Offset Source Candidate Audit

Gate 585 tests whether the charged-lepton chamber-wall offset `epsilon_e=0.039569756309433 rad` matches any typed dimensionless runtime quantity.  The nearest candidate is the loop scale `1/(8*pi)=0.0397887357729738`, with relative residual `0.00553401092057273`, so it is recorded as a near loop-sized clue but not certified as a source.  Electroweak couplings, boundary loop-normalized couplings, strong mismatch, scalar residual, weak-angle residual, and CKM area proxies do not fix `epsilon_e`.

Verdict: `CONDITIONAL_SUPPORT_BEST_SOURCE_CANDIDATE_IS_ONE_OVER_8PI_LOOP_SCALE`; `CONDITIONAL_SUPPORT_ONE_OVER_8PI_NEAR_EPSILON_BUT_NOT_CERTIFIED`; `FAILED_ROUTE_NO_DIMENSIONLESS_RUNTIME_CANDIDATE_CERTIFIED_AS_EPSILON_SOURCE`; `FAILED_ROUTE_NO_NATIVE_LOOP_FACTOR_TO_KOIDE_WALL_OFFSET_OPERATOR`; `FIREWALL_PRESERVED_GATE585_SOURCE_CANDIDATE_BOUNDARY`.

### Gate 586 — Koide Loop-Angle Deficit Audit

Gate 586 factors the Gate 585 loop-sized wall-offset relation as `epsilon_e=(1/(8*pi))(1-kappa_e)`.  It computes `kappa_e=0.00550355419157456` and tests typed runtime correction candidates. ## Gate 600 — Charged-Lepton Root-Extension Branch and Chamber Monodromy Audit

Gate 600 decomposes the Gate 599 algebraic-over-trace-ring result into explicit branch data. The native trace ring gives `chi_e(lambda)` and therefore an unordered cubic spectrum. The cubic splitting field is typed as `K_e=Frac(R_e)(lambda_1,lambda_2,lambda_3)`, with discriminant `Delta_e=prod_{i<j}(lambda_i-lambda_j)^2` and generic `S_3` monodromy unless extra square-discriminant data are supplied. Current ASHA data gives no native eigenvalue branch, charged-lepton ordering, positive fourth-root theorem, Fourier cyclic chamber selector, or electron-zero wall selector.

The minimal sealed datum is therefore `ChargedLeptonRootBranchChamberSeal`: a cubic splitting branch `(lambda_e,lambda_mu,lambda_tau)`, positive fourth roots `x_i=lambda_i^(1/4)`, the canonical `(e,mu,tau)` chamber, the Fourier cyclic order, and the electron-zero wall defining `epsilon(H_e)`. Thus `B_flav` is branch-anchored as `1-8*pi*epsilon_branch(R_e)-(1/4)Tr(P_eP_3^nu)+J(H_u,H_d)`, but remains environmental.

Verdict: `PASS_CUBIC_SPLITTING_FIELD_TYPED`; `PASS_DISCRIMINANT_AND_MONODROMY_DATA_TYPED`; `CONDITIONAL_SUPPORT_EPSILON_H_E_BRANCH_ALGEBRAIC_OVER_TRACE_RING`; `CONDITIONAL_SUPPORT_CHARGED_LEPTON_ROOT_BRANCH_CHAMBER_SEAL_DEFINED`; `FAILED_ROUTE_TRACE_RING_DOES_NOT_SELECT_CHARGED_LEPTON_ORDERING`; `FAILED_ROUTE_NO_NATIVE_POSITIVE_FOURTH_ROOT_BRANCH_THEOREM`; `FAILED_ROUTE_NO_NATIVE_ELECTRON_WALL_OR_CHAMBER_SELECTOR`; `FAILED_ROUTE_B_FLAV_REMAINS_ENVIRONMENTAL`; `FIREWALL_PRESERVED_GATE600_BRANCH_CHAMBER_MONODROMY_BOUNDARY`.

## Gate 601 — Flavor Branch-Compatibility Selector Audit

Gate 601 tests whether the environmental balance `B_flav(sigma,i,s_J)=1-8*pi*epsilon_sigma(H_e)-(1/4)Tr(P_eP_i^nu)+s_J J_CKM` selects the observed branch among charged-lepton chamber permutations, neutrino projectors, and CKM orientation signs. The observed branch `(e,mu,tau), i=3, s_J=+1` lies in the minimal residual class with `|B_flav|≈2.77587313788957e-06`.

The audit finds that the balance strongly selects the third neutrino projector `P_3^nu` and the positive CKM orientation sign. However, when `epsilon_sigma` is measured relative to the physical electron-zero wall, all six charged-lepton permutations have the same wall offset and therefore form a sixfold minimal class. Thus the balance is a strong environmental compatibility filter, but not a unique charged-lepton branch selector.

## Gate 602 — Unsealed Lepton-Wall / PMNS-Row Branch Selector Audit

Gate 602 removes the electron-wall preselection from Gate 601 by allowing the charged-lepton wall label and PMNS row to vary. It evaluates `B_flav(sigma,alpha,i,s_J)=1-8*pi*epsilon_{sigma,alpha}(H_e)-(1/4)Tr(P_alphaP_i^nu)+s_JJ_CKM` over six charged-lepton branches, three lepton wall/PMNS row labels, three neutrino projectors, and two CKM signs.

The minimal residual class contains the observed tuple `(alpha=e, i=3, s_J=+1)` with `|B_flav|≈2.77587314e-06`; all minimal rows have the electron row, third neutrino projector, and positive CKM orientation sign. The remaining degeneracy is only the sixfold charged-lepton sigma/cyclic ordering. Thus `B_flav` selects the electron wall/PMNS row, `P_3^nu`, and `+J_CKM` environmentally, but still does not provide a full native branch-selection theorem.

Verdict: `CONDITIONAL_SUPPORT_BALANCE_SELECTS_ELECTRON_ROW`; `CONDITIONAL_SUPPORT_BALANCE_SELECTS_P3_NU_AND_POSITIVE_CKM_SIGN`; `CONDITIONAL_SUPPORT_CHARGED_LEPTON_SIGMA_DEGENERACY_REMAINS`; `FAILED_ROUTE_FULL_CHARGED_LEPTON_ORDERING_NOT_UNIQUELY_SELECTED`; `FAILED_ROUTE_NO_NATIVE_BRANCH_SELECTION_THEOREM`; `FIREWALL_PRESERVED_GATE602_UNSEALED_LEPTON_WALL_BOUNDARY`.

## Gate 603 — Charged-Lepton Sigma Degeneracy Gauge-or-Orientation Audit

Gate 603 audits the sixfold charged-lepton sigma/cyclic-order degeneracy left by Gate 602. The result is that `B_flav` uses the unsigned electron-wall distance after `alpha=e` is selected, so it does not see the cyclic orientation of the Fourier chamber. The observed balance therefore selects the electron wall, `P_3^nu`, and `+J_CKM`, but sigma remains a Fourier-coordinate redundancy for the balance itself.

If full charged-lepton cyclic/order orientation is treated as physical, the missing datum is a signed Vandermonde/discriminant orientation seal, such as `sgn(V_e)` or `sgn(V_x)`. The native trace ring supplies only the symmetric discriminant `Delta_e=V_e^2`, not the sign.

## Gate 606 — Boundary-to-Endpoint RG Threshold Transport Spine Audit

Gate 606 follows the Gate 605 master history-seal vector and audits the practical boundary-to-endpoint transport spine. It classifies the native ASHA boundary structures `k_Y=5/3`, `sin²(theta_*)=3/8`, `g1=g2`, the symbolic electroweak Hessian, finite algebra sockets, scalar doublet socket, and the native polynomial spectral-action trace cable separately from endpoint ledgers and threshold-history data.

The current runtime supplies a one-loop `g1=g2` crossing at `Lambda_12≈9.72424831265293e13 GeV`, with `g_star≈0.5377817790927929`, `g3(Lambda_12)≈0.5652050934199595`, `Delta_3≈-0.32739043299998416`, and `R_3≈1.0509933868964996`. This is a gauge transport residual requiring threshold or higher-loop history; it is not a full unification theorem. The scalar v1 ledger gives `lambda(M_Z)≈0.1296525650504758`, `lambda(Lambda_12)≈-0.049700942077683274`, and a zero crossing near `2.5759272046129573e6 GeV`, but this is explicitly one-loop/top-dominant and not a final vacuum-stability claim.

Gate 606 defines correction slots `delta_i^gauge`, `delta_lambda`, `delta_yukawa`, `delta_K_phi`, `delta_v`, `delta_pole_MSbar`, and `delta_boundary`, while preserving the kinetic-scale, VEV, endpoint, flavor, and product-time firewalls. The RG parameter is a transport scale, not Lorentzian time or OS/Hilbert dynamics.

Verdict: `PASS_GAUGE_RG_TRANSPORT_SLOTS_DEFINED`; `PASS_SCALAR_RG_TRANSPORT_SLOTS_DEFINED`; `PASS_THRESHOLD_CORRECTION_LEDGER_DEFINED`; `CONDITIONAL_SUPPORT_RG_THRESHOLD_TRANSPORT_IS_NEXT_ACTIONABLE_HISTORY_SPINE`; `FAILED_ROUTE_NO_NATIVE_RG_THRESHOLD_THEOREM`; `FAILED_ROUTE_NO_ABSOLUTE_KINETIC_SCALE`; `FAILED_ROUTE_NO_HIGGS_VEV_DERIVATION`; `FIREWALL_PRESERVED_RG_SCALE_NOT_PRODUCT_TIME`; `FIREWALL_PRESERVED_GATE606_RG_THRESHOLD_TRANSPORT_SPINE_BOUNDARY`.

## Gate 607 — Strong-Coupling Threshold Residual Ledger Audit

Gate 607 converts the Gate 606 strong-coupling mismatch at `Lambda_12` into coupling, inverse-coupling, alpha-inverse, threshold-slot, beta-deformation, and meeting-scale-triangle ledgers. It finds `delta_3^threshold=0.32739043299998416` in `u=1/g^2` convention, or `Delta alpha_3^{-1}=4.11410951667333`, as the positive correction required to bring `g3(Lambda_12)` down to `g_star`.

The one-loop meeting-scale triangle is `Lambda_12≈9.724e13 GeV`, `Lambda_13≈9.983e14 GeV`, and `Lambda_23≈8.250e16 GeV`, so the v1 runtime certifies only the `g1=g2` boundary-normalization test. A constant one-loop diagnostic deformation would require `Delta b3≈-0.93336`, about `13.3%` of `|b3_SM|`, but this is not promoted to a model.

Verdict: `PASS_STRONG_RESIDUAL_CONVERTED_IN_MULTIPLE_SCHEMES`; `PASS_STRONG_THRESHOLD_SLOT_DEFINED`; `CONDITIONAL_SUPPORT_REQUIRED_STRONG_THRESHOLD_LEDGER_QUANTIFIED`; `CONDITIONAL_SUPPORT_BETA_DEFORMATION_SIZE_COMPUTED`; `PASS_MEETING_SCALE_TRIANGLE_COMPUTED_ONE_LOOP`; `FAILED_ROUTE_NO_NATIVE_STRONG_THRESHOLD_THEOREM`; `FAILED_ROUTE_NO_FULL_GAUGE_UNIFICATION_CLAIM`; `FIREWALL_PRESERVED_GATE607_STRONG_THRESHOLD_RESIDUAL_BOUNDARY`.

## Gate 608 — Gauge Meeting-Scale Triangle Geometry Audit

Gate 608 treats the one-loop pairwise gauge crossing scales as a transport-ledger triangle, not as a unification theorem.  The triangle is `Lambda_12≈9.724e13 GeV`, `Lambda_13≈9.983e14 GeV`, and `Lambda_23≈8.250e16 GeV`, spanning `2.9286` decades.  The log-geometric diagnostic scale is `Lambda_geom≈2.0007e15 GeV`, but no native theorem selects it as a physical boundary.

Boundary choices leave different residuals: at `Lambda_12`, `g1=g2` is exact and `u3-u*≈-0.32739`; at `Lambda_13`, `g1=g3` is exact and `u2-u13≈0.21433`; at `Lambda_23`, `g2=g3` is exact and `u1-u23≈-0.62062`.  Beta-deformation rows are recorded only as diagnostics, including the Gate 607 `Delta b3≈-0.93336` strong-sector deformation and a minimal-norm deformation at `Lambda_geom`.

Verdict: `PASS_LOG_TRIANGLE_GEOMETRY_COMPUTED`; `PASS_BOUNDARY_CHOICE_RESIDUALS_CLASSIFIED`; `PASS_BETA_DEFORMATION_VECTOR_AUDIT_DEFINED`; `CONDITIONAL_SUPPORT_MEETING_TRIANGLE_IS_STRUCTURED_TRANSPORT_LEDGER`; `FAILED_ROUTE_NO_SINGLE_ONE_LOOP_UNIFICATION_POINT`; `FAILED_ROUTE_NO_NATIVE_THRESHOLD_OR_EXTRA_FIELD_THEOREM`; `FIREWALL_PRESERVED_GATE608_MEETING_TRIANGLE_BOUNDARY`.

## Gate 609 — Strong Threshold Sign and Field-Content Viability Audit

Gate 609 follows the meeting-scale triangle by auditing the sign of the correction needed to close the strong-sector residual at `Lambda_12`.  The required inverse-coupling correction is `delta_3^threshold≈0.32739043299998416`, or `Delta alpha_3^{-1}≈4.11410951667333`.  As a full-interval one-loop diagnostic this corresponds to `Delta b3≈-0.933360651351616`, making `b3_eff≈-7.93336065135162`.
